RAG-HAR+ uses an offline Retrieval Designer Agent to design dataset-specific feature groups from a diverse pool of motion descriptors, enabling sensor windows to be compared using features better aligned with dataset-specific activity patterns. During inference, RAG-HAR+ uses majority voting over retrieved neighbors for samples with strong retrieval evidence and defers only uncertain cases to an LLM-based Ambiguity Resolver Agent. Across six HAR benchmarks, RAG-HAR+ maintains competitive or improved performance while reducing LLM usage, token consumption, and inference time. We further extend the RAG-HAR mobile prototype to demonstrate the practical feasibility of retrieval-first, LLM-assisted HAR in mobile sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [174] [AIGen: Automating AI Bill of Materials Generation Through Hybrid MLOps Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26652)
*Federica Pepe,Daniele Bifolco,Costantino Martignetti,Aureliano D'Amici,Fabiano Izzo,Damian A. Tamburri,Massimiliano Di Penta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIGen，一个模块化、符合SPDX 3.0标准的人工智能物料清单（AIBoM）生成工具，基于MLflow MLOps框架，结合挖掘启发式与大型语言模型，支持插件扩展，旨在促进AI供应链治理与法规合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的负責任开发与部署需要对数据集、模型权重、训练流程和运行时依赖等组成工件进行严格文档化。尽管SPDX 3.0标准原生支持AI和数据配置文件，但缺乏能够自动化、可扩展地生成符合标准AIBoM的实用工具。

Method: AIGen基于MLflow MLOps框架，结合挖掘启发式算法与大型语言模型（LLM）来自动生成AIBoM。采用插件接口设计，允许用户在无需修改核心代码库的情况下扩展领域特定收集器，支持Hugging Face、PyTorch和TensorFlow等多种AI框架。

Result: AIGen能够生成符合SPDX 3.0 AI配置文件的机器可读、可互操作的AI系统组件清单。工具已开源并提供演示视频。

Conclusion: AIGen为透明、可问责的AI供应链治理提供了具体、可复用的基础，有助于满足欧盟AI法案、NIST AI风险管理框架和ISO/IEC 42001等合规要求。

Abstract: The responsible development and deployment of artificial intelligence (AI) systems requires rigorous documentation of their constituent artifacts, e.g., datasets, model weights, training pipelines, and runtime dependencies. Although the Software Package Data Exchange (SPDX) 3.0 standard introduced native support for AI and dataset profiles, practical tooling capable of generating standards-compliant AI Bills of Materials (AIBoMs) in an automated and extensible manner remains scarce. This paper presents AIGen, a modular AIBoM generator that produces machine-readable, interoperable inventories of AI system components that comply with the SPDX 3.0 AI profile. AIGen works on top of the MLflow MLOps framework and combines mining heuristics with Large Language Models to generate AIBoMs. A plugin interface allows practitioners to extend the tool with domain-specific collectors without modifying the core codebase, supporting heterogeneous AI frameworks such as Hugging Face, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. AIGen is designed to facilitate compliance with the European Union AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework, and ISO/IEC 42001, providing a concrete, reusable foundation for transparent, accountable AI supply chain governance. Tool URL: https://github.com/danielebifolco/AIGen Tool Video: https://youtu.be/\_nAbXDWfVL4

</details>


### [175] [Efficient Heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization for Risk-Aware AutoRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26680)
*Mingxuan Che,Tsung-Yuan Tseng,Theresa Eimer,Marius Lindauer,Alexander von Rohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ERAHBO的贝叶斯优化方法，通过异方差建模同时优化强化学习超参数配置的平均回报和方差，并采用自适应重采样提升样本效率，实验表明该方法在风险规避场景下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习结果高度随机，且超参数配置对期望性能和变异性都有显著影响，现有方法缺乏对风险（回报方差）的高效优化。

Method: 采用异方差贝叶斯优化，同时建模学习结果的均值和方差作为超参数的函数；通过自适应重采样（而非固定预算）提升超参数优化的样本效率。

Result: 在多种RL算法和环境上的实验表明，ERAHBO在样本效率和风险规避回报方面普遍优于风险中性及风险规避基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效识别高平均回报且低变异性的超参数配置，同时提升了超参数优化的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success across a wide range of complex tasks. However, RL outcomes can be highly stochastic, and both expected performance and variability often depend on hyperparameter (HP) configurations. We propose efficient and risk-averse heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization (ERAHBO), a Bayesian optimization method that models both the mean and variance of learning outcomes as functions of the HP configurations. ERAHBO aims to identify HP configurations that achieve high average return while reducing variability across training runs, and it improves the sample efficiency of the HP optimization via adaptive re-sampling rather than a fixed budget per HP. Empirical evaluations across diverse RL algorithms and environments demonstrate that ERAHBO generally outperforms both risk-neutral and risk-averse baselines, delivering improved sample efficiency for risk-averse returns.

</details>


### [176] [Mixture-of-experts for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26708)
*Florent Imbert,Eric Anquetil,Yann Soullard,Romain Tavenard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家模型(MOE)的数字笔在线手写轨迹重建方法，专门处理触摸和悬停两种不同轨迹的重建任务，并发布了新的公开基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔可以在任何表面书写并保留数字轨迹，但其手写轨迹重建精度不足，特别是如何准确定位下一次触摸笔迹的悬停轨迹分析尚待改善。作者希望提升手写轨迹重建的精细度，以应用于课堂辅助书写学习等场景。

Method: 采用混合专家(MOE)架构，包含两个专家模型：触摸专家模型（重建接触轨迹）和悬停专家模型（分析笔尖悬停轨迹以准确定位下一笔触摸位置）。每个专家基于额外上下文或特定样本进行针对性学习优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在重建性能上显著优于其主要竞争对手。

Conclusion: 提出的MOE方法能有效提升数字笔在线手写轨迹重建精度，并提供了新的公开基准数据集以推动该领域的研究。

Abstract: The use of digital pens for online handwriting trajectory reconstruction is a prevalent method for human-computer interaction. In this study, we focus on a digital pen equipped with sensors where we aim at reconstructing the online handwriting trajectory. This pen enables writing on any surface and preserving the digital trace of handwriting. This type of pen could be used as an aid to learning to write in classroom. In this paper, we propose a new approach learning to finely reconstruct the touching trajectories while precisely analyzing the hovering part in order to position the next touching trace correctly. This relies on a Mixture-Of-Experts (MOE) approach. The first expert is dedicated for the pencil touch, and is named touching expert model. The second one is dedicated for the hovering pen trajectory, and is named hovering expert model. We improve on the learning of each of these experts based on additional context or specific examples. In addition we introduce a novel public benchmark dataset, to enable future research and comparisons in the field of handwriting reconstruction. The results demonstrates a significant enhancement compared to its primary competitors.

</details>


### [177] [PowerAtlas: Towards Electricity-Computing Co-Scheduling for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26710)
*Kaiwen Jiang,Siya Xu,Ziyue Zhu,Chao Yang,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerAtlas是一个面向数据中心电力-计算协同调度的LLM智能体框架，能够同时满足电网运行约束和计算任务SLA，基于真实电网数据构建了ECBench基准测试，实验证明其在可行性和成本上优于多种大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载快速增长使数据中心成为大规模、波动性强但时空灵活的电网负荷，迫切需要电力-计算的协同调度。然而通用大语言模型在严格的电网约束下生成的调度方案往往不可行，会导致线路潮流越限和负荷损失。

Method: 提出PowerAtlas框架，集成历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，以LLM智能体形式生成同时满足电网运行规则和计算任务SLA的联合决策。与中国省级电力公司合作构建实验性电力-计算网络，基于真实数据中心数据验证；从脱敏运行数据构建包含2000个调度实例的ECBench基准，并提供oracle最优解。

Result: 在11种大语言模型上的实验表明，PowerAtlas在真实物理运行条件下有效，在三个开放权重基座模型上均保持一致的可行性和成本收益。

Conclusion: PowerAtlas成功将LLM应用于电力-计算协同调度这一高约束工程问题，通过注入领域知识和物理约束显著提升了调度方案的可行性，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is turning data centers into large-scale, volatile, yet spatiotemporally flexible grid loads, creating an urgent need for coordinated electricity-computing scheduling. Under stringent grid constraints, schedules from general-purpose large language models (LLMs) are often infeasible, causing line-flow violations and unserved load. We present PowerAtlas, an LLM-agent framework for electricity-computing co-scheduling that integrates historical instances, domain knowledge, and physical constraints to produce joint decisions satisfying both grid operational rules and the service-level agreements (SLAs) of computing tasks. Working with a provincial power utility in China, we built an experimental electricity-computing network and validated the decision loop on real data-center data; from de-identified operational data we further constructed ECBench, a benchmark of 2,000 scheduling instances with oracle-optimal solutions. Experiments across eleven LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of PowerAtlas under realistic physical operating conditions, with consistent feasibility and cost gains across three open-weight backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/JAVA-Jiang/PowerAtlas.

</details>


### [178] [Domain adaptation for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26736)
*Florent Imbert,Romain Tavenard,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于运动传感器数字笔在手写轨迹重建中的域适应方法，解决成人与儿童手写信号差异问题，比较了域适应、从头训练和微调三种方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔在儿童书写学习中具有重要教育价值，但成人与儿童在手写速度和自信度上的差异导致传感器信号存在巨大差异，影响轨迹重建的准确性。

Method: 采用域适应方法构建统一的中间特征表示，以实现跨年龄段的手写轨迹重建，并与从头训练和微调两种方法进行对比。

Result: 域适应方法在利用已有知识迁移至不同应用场景方面展现出优势，优于从头训练和简单微调方法。

Conclusion: 域适应方法能有效弥合成人与儿童手写信号差异，提升数字笔轨迹重建的泛化能力，可作为教育场景中书写学习辅助工具的关键技术。

Abstract: Digital pens are commonly used to write on digital devices, providing the handwriting trace and enhancing human-computer interation. This study focuses on a digital pen equipped with kinematic sensors, allowing users to write on any surface while simultaneously preserving a digital trajectory of handwriting. This technology holds significant potential as a valuable educational tool, particularly in classrooms where it can facilitate the process of learning to write. A major issue is based on the difference in captured signals between adults and children. For similar handwriting trace, we have large differences in sensor signals due to differences in speed and confidence in the handwriting gesture of children. To address this, we investigate a domain adaptation approach to build a unified intermediate feature representation aimed at facilitating the trajectory reconstruction. We demonstrate the interest of domain adaptation methods in leveraging existing knowledge for application in different contexts. Specifically, we compare our domain adaptation approach with two other methods: training the model from scratch and fine-tuning the model.

</details>


### [179] [CalTwin: Towards Calibrated, Shift-Robust Medical World Models via Fisher-Information Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26752)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalTwin，一种统一的正则化目标，通过Fisher信息量移位惩罚和置信度错位惩罚，解决医学世界模型中的协变量移位和置信度错位问题。在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症数据集上，CalTwin将OOD下一步潜状态MSE降低了9.1%，ECE降低0.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学世界模型在临床部署中存在两个关键故障模式：(i)协变量移位——训练数据分散在不同医院、扫描仪和时间段，导致潜状态预测器看到的特征分布不一致；(ii)置信度错位——多步预测在临床风险最高处往往过度自信。现有方法分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出CalTwin正则化目标，包含两个组件：(1)基于Fisher信息量的移位惩罚（FIM penalty），源自作者先前对碎片化协变量移位修复的工作；(2)置信度错位惩罚（CMP），源自作者先前对校准视觉-语言分类的工作。将两者应用于基于GRU的医学世界模型的潜状态转移预测器。在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症挑战数据集上评估，将两个医院系统作为顺序训练碎片，未见系统作为分布外测试集。

Result: CalTwin将OOD下一步潜状态MSE降低9.1%（相对于无惩罚基线），其中FIM惩罚单独贡献7.0%；置信度错位惩罚带来的ECE降低（0.7%）虽真实但较小，CMP单独贡献1.3%。

Conclusion: 通过统一的正则化框架可以同时缓解医学世界模型中的协变量移位和置信度错位问题。FIM惩罚在减少预测误差方面贡献显著，而CMP在置信度校准上提供了额外但较小的改进。该工作为将校准技术从分类设置迁移到潜空间预测设置提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Medical world models aim to learn a latent state of patient or organ physiology and a transition function that forecasts how that state evolves under interventions, supporting downstream tasks from imaging-based diagnosis to digital-twin treatment planning. Two failure modes threaten the reliability of such models in clinical deployment: (i)~\emph{covariate shift}, because training data are fragmented across hospitals, scanners, and time, so the feature distribution seen by the latent-dynamics predictor differs across fragments and from the distribution at deployment; and (ii)~\emph{confidence misalignment}, because multi-step forecasts are often overconfident exactly where clinical risk is highest. We argue that both problems admit a unified treatment via a single lightweight regularisation objective, \textbf{CalTwin}, which combines a Fisher-Information-based shift penalty adapted from our prior work on fragmented covariate-shift remediation~\cite{khan2025mitigating,khan2025causal} with a Confidence Misalignment Penalty adapted from our prior work on calibrated vision-language classification~\cite{khan2025confidence}, applied here to a GRU-based medical world model's latent transition predictor. We derive the combined objective, establish which proof steps transfer from the classification setting without modification and which require adaptation, and evaluate it on the PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge, treating the two hospital systems as sequential training fragments and the unseen system as an out-of-distribution test. CalTwin reduces OOD next-step latent-state MSE by 9.1\% relative to the no-penalty baseline (FIM penalty alone accounts for 7.0\%); the ECE reduction from the Confidence Misalignment Penalty is real but small (0.7\% for CalTwin, 1.3\% for CMP alone).

</details>


### [180] [Journey Operators for Structured Multi-Axis Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26775)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个处理多轴结构数据的统一框架，证明了当轴变换可交换时路径无关性成立，推导了RoPE类方法的必然性，并设计了JoFormer模型，在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轴结构数据（如图像、音频、3D体积）中，沿同一轴顺序重要，但跨独立轴的操作（组合与移动）应独立于轴的顺序。现有方法缺乏对这种多轴结构的统一建模框架。

Method: 1) 提出多轴变换框架：每个数据项携带内容及每个轴的变换；2) 定义路径与旅程算子为沿路径的逐轴变换乘积；3) 证明路径无关性当且仅当轴变换可交换；4) 在对称性、上循环性、双线性及范数保持假设下推导出块状旋转形式的评分规则；5) 设计JoFormer模型用于值聚合。

Result: 1) 恢复RoPE及其多维变体为特例（变换固定时）；2) 数据依赖变换时可获得内容自适应的位置归纳偏置；3) 证明路径定义良好的充要条件（轴变换可交换）；4) 推导出RoPE类方法必然性的理论依据；5) JoFormer在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上的初步实验显示效果。

Conclusion: 该框架统一解释了RoPE类方法的理论基础，为设计具有内容自适应位置归纳偏置的模型提供了新方向，JoFormer与注意力和状态空间模型相关，展示了这些归纳偏置在实际中的可观影响。

Abstract: Many kinds of data have structure along one or more axes: words in a sentence, pixels in an image, nodes in a tree, frames in audio, or cells in a 3D volume. Along one axis, order matters: "the dog bit the man" is different from "the man bit the dog." Across independent axes, however, neither composition nor movement should depend on the order of axes: in an image, composing right then down should give the same result as composing down then right, and moving right then down should describe the same relative position as moving down then right.
  We develop a framework for modeling this kind of multi-axis structure. Each data item carries its content together with a small transformation for each axis. A path connecting two positions defines a journey; the journey operator is the product of per-axis transformations along that path, governing both how data composes along the path and how relative position is described. When the transformations are fixed, our framework recovers Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and its multi-dimensional variants. When they depend on the data, the model gains a content-adaptive positional inductive bias.
  We show exactly when these paths are well-defined: both composition and movement across axes are path-independent precisely when the axis transformations commute. We also prove that, under the stated toral-frame symmetry, cocycle, bilinearity, and norm-preservation assumptions, the resulting pairwise scoring rule must take the form of block-wise rotations, explaining why RoPE-like methods arise naturally.
  Finally, we use this theory to design JoFormer, a model for value aggregation, and relate it to attention and state-space models (SSMs).
  Initial experiments across vision, language, and length generalization suggest that these inductive biases can have observable consequences in
  practice.

</details>


### [181] [SkillRise: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Cross-Task Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26784)
*Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Zishan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Zhengzhou Cai,Kangning Zhang,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Ziang Ye,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiwen Liu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillRise是一个统一的强化学习框架，让LLM智能体在跨任务场景中通过单一策略交替执行任务解决与技能文档编排，实现可迁移技能的提取、优化与复用，在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上相比最强基线提升2.3-8.5个百分点，且测试时随关联任务序列长度增加性能持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体强化学习方法将任务视为独立片段，无法利用相关任务间共享的可复用解决方案模式；而现有的技能学习方法要么聚焦于单任务的重复尝试，要么采用多阶段流水线将技能提取、检索和执行纠缠在一起，缺乏统一高效的跨任务技能学习框架。

Method: 提出SkillRise框架：将相关实例组织为渐进式难度序列，使用单一策略交替执行任务解决与技能文档编排，将演化的技能文档直接传递给下一任务；通过解耦的跨任务信用分配机制，用当前任务结果监督解决行为，用折现的下游结果监督编排行为。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，SkillRise取得最强的Pass@1性能，相比最强基线提升2.3-8.5个百分点；其学习的编排策略在相同任务的重复尝试中同样有效；测试时随关联任务序列长度增加性能持续提升，且显著降低了多阶段技能学习管线的运行时开销。

Conclusion: SkillRise提供了一种简单高效的训练范式，使LLM智能体能够跨任务提取、优化和复用可迁移技能，其性能提升源于技能复用而非同一任务的重复采样，并能在降低计算开销的同时保持强性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents often encounter related yet distinct tasks that share reusable solution patterns. Yet standard agentic reinforcement learning treats tasks as independent episodes, while existing approaches to skill learning either focus on repeated attempts of one task or use pipelines with multiple stages that entangle extraction, retrieval, and execution. We introduce SkillRise, a unified reinforcement learning framework for learning skills across tasks. SkillRise organizes related instances into progressively challenging sequences and uses a single policy to alternate between task solving and curating an evolving skill document passed directly to the next task. Decoupled credit assignment across tasks supervises solving with the current task outcome and curation with discounted downstream outcomes. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show that SkillRise achieves the strongest Pass@1 performance among the compared methods, with gains over the strongest baseline ranging from 2.3 to 8.5 percentage points. Although trained across distinct tasks, its learned curation policy remains effective for repeated attempts on the same task. Further analysis reveals scaling at test time across tasks: performance improves with longer sequences of related tasks even when each task is attempted only once. This trend suggests that SkillRise reuses transferable skills across tasks rather than benefiting from repeated sampling of the same task. SkillRise further retains strong performance while substantially reducing the runtime overhead of skill learning pipelines with multiple stages. Together, these results provide a simple and efficient training paradigm for LLM agents to extract, refine, and reuse transferable skills across tasks.

</details>


### [182] [FedTopo: Relation-Level Topology Sharing for Model-Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26801)
*Zhaoyang Ma,Zhihao Wu,Xin Gao,Lipo Wang,Youfang Lin,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTopo提出了一种基于类关系拓扑的联邦学习框架，通过传递类间关系而非绝对表征空间来解决异构骨干网络下的知识共享问题，在三个数据集上优于参数共享、蒸馏共享和原型共享基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法（参数共享、蒸馏预测、类原型）将全局知识编码在绝对空间中，当客户端使用异构骨干网络时，表征空间不对齐导致共享知识不可靠，误导本地训练。

Method: 每个客户端从本地原型构建类关系拓扑（描述类间关系），连同类别统计信息上传至服务器；服务器以可靠性感知方式聚合拓扑（弱支持拓扑降权），广播全局拓扑至客户端；客户端利用全局拓扑指导本地训练，强调拓扑相似的负类。

Result: 在三个数据集、八种异构骨干网络下，FedTopo在性能上一致优于参数共享、蒸馏共享和原型共享基线，且通信开销低、无推理开销。

Conclusion: 类关系拓扑作为一种关系级知识传递方式，有效解决了异构联邦学习中的表征空间不对齐问题，兼具高性能与低通信成本。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning over decentralized data silos without centralizing raw data. However, heterogeneous local architectures often induce non-aligned representation spaces, making it difficult to transfer global knowledge across silos. Existing paradigms share this knowledge as model parameters, distilled predictions, or class prototypes, yet all encode it in an absolute space that must be aligned across clients. Heterogeneous backbones break this alignment, so the shared knowledge becomes unreliable and misleads local training. We propose FedTopo, a relation-level framework that encodes global knowledge as class relation topology, capturing how classes relate within each client rather than where they lie in feature space. Each client builds its relation topology from local prototypes and uploads it with class statistics. The server then aggregates these relations in a reliability-aware manner that down-weights weakly supported ones, and broadcasts the global topology to clients. The global topology guides local training by emphasizing topology-similar negative classes. Experiments on three datasets under eight heterogeneous backbones show that FedTopo consistently outperforms parameter-, distillation-, and prototype-sharing baselines, with low communication and no inference overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zhaoyang-Ma/FedTopo.

</details>


### [183] [Forecasting Trajectory-Level Safety Risks in Black-Box Multi-Turn Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26820)
*Shi Lin,Peng Qian,Dinghao Liu,Renjie Sun,Sifan Wu,Dezhang Kong,Chenpei Wang,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Recast框架，将LLM安全防护从单轮违规检测扩展到轨迹级风险预测，通过双尺度轨迹视图检索证据、建模组合风险演化，并利用因果时序编码器预测未来风险出现轮次。实验覆盖7类风险，成功预测88.3%的未来安全故障，平均提前2.41轮，误报率12.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全防护机制是反应式的，只能检测已发生的违规行为，无法在长期多轮交互中预测潜在风险的演化过程，缺乏主动预防能力。

Method: Recast框架包含三个核心模块：1）双尺度轨迹视图，从短期对话进展和长期历史上下文中检索风险相关证据；2）组合风险演化建模，捕获当前风险配置及其时间动态；3）因果时序编码器，学习潜在风险演化模式并预测未来风险出现的轮次分布。

Result: 在7类风险类别上，Recast预测出88.3%的未来安全故障，平均提前2.41轮预警，误报率为12.3%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级风险预测能够有效在安全违规发生前识别新兴风险，证明了从单轮检测向轨迹级预测转变的有效性，为LLM安全防护提供了前瞻性解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve from standalone assistants into autonomous agents, ensuring their safety requires shifting beyond pointwise risk assessment to understand how risks emerge and unfold over long-horizon trajectories. In multi-turn interactions, malicious intent can be decomposed across seemingly harmless turns and gradually reconstructed through interaction trajectories, eventually resulting in safety failures. Existing safeguards remain largely reactive, detecting manifested violations while lacking the ability to predict latent risk evolution and enable preemptive prevention. To address this limitation, we propose Recast, a safety risk forecasting framework that advances LLM safeguarding beyond turn-level violation detection to trajectory-level risk prediction. Recast first retrieves risk-relevant evidence from both short-term dialogue progression and long-term historical context via a dual-scale trajectory view. It then models compositional risk evolution by capturing the current risk configuration and its temporal dynamics. Finally, a causal temporal encoder learns latent risk evolution patterns and predicts the distribution of future risk emergence turns. Extensive experiments across 7 risk categories show that Recast predicts 88.3% of future safety failures with an average lead time of 2.41 turns, while maintaining a false alarm rate of 12.3%, showcasing the effectiveness of trajectory-level forecasting in identifying emerging risks before safety violations occur.

</details>


### [184] [Budget-Aware LLM Discovery via Cost-Calibrated Frontier Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26828)
*Yansen Zhang,Yilu Liu,Tianyu Liu,Jiamin Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Kai Xie,Xue Liu,Chen Ma,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CostAda方法，通过成本校准的前沿效用函数来指导搜索过程中的信用分配，在固定token预算下实现更高效的科学发现搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应搜索控制器仅基于分数进步分配信用，忽略了不同搜索动作（如提示长度、重试、引导调用）在token成本上的差异，导致在成本多样化的前沿搜索中无法有效分配有限预算。

Method: 提出CostAda，核心是成本校准的前沿效用函数，将前沿进展相对于实际动作成本进行量化，并基于剩余预算调节信用分配。该信号控制局部探索强度、前沿分配和预算感知的战术干预。

Result: 在16个基准-骨干对中的12个上，以最多一半的预算达到最强基线全预算质量；在GLM-5和GPT-5.4下所有8个基准上均达到最强平均最终质量。

Conclusion: 成本与剩余预算应塑造搜索过程本身而非仅作为会计变量或停止规则，CostAda验证了成本校准信用分配能显著提升预算受限下的搜索效率。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly support scientific and algorithmic discovery through inference-time search over evaluated candidates. Existing adaptive discovery controllers assign credit based only on score progress, even though prompt length, retries, and guidance calls cause search actions to incur different token costs. We prove that cost-blind credit can forfeit all but a vanishing fraction of attainable quality as frontiers multiply and costs diverge. Under a fixed search-side token budget, the controller must decide which frontier is improving and whether its gain justifies the realized cost before the budget is exhausted. We introduce \textbf{CostAda}, a cost-calibrated adaptive controller built around \emph{cost-calibrated frontier utility}. The utility values frontier progress relative to realized action cost and conditions that credit on the remaining budget. CostAda uses this signal to control local exploration intensity, frontier allocation, and budgeted tactic intervention. Cost and remaining budget therefore shape the search rather than serving only as accounting variables or a stopping rule. CostAda reaches the strongest baseline's full-budget quality with at most half the budget on twelve of sixteen benchmark--backbone pairs while achieving the strongest mean final quality on all eight benchmarks under GLM-5 and GPT-5.4.

</details>


### [185] [Kairos: Numerically Robust News Recommendation under Item Cold-Start via Cholesky-based LinUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26832)
*Finn Hertsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对区域新闻数据稀疏和短TTL导致的冷启动问题，提出Kairos框架，通过LinUCB上下文在线学习、Cholesky秩1更新（替代Sherman-Morrison）保证数值稳定性，结合Matryoshka表示学习降低推理延迟，在Tagesschau API上实现4.85倍效率提升且不显著损失排序精度，为资源受限环境下的推荐系统提供了蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习推荐模型依赖海量交互数据，但区域新闻个性化场景存在结构性冷启动问题：新闻TTL短（<48小时）、文章池浅，导致协同过滤因数据匮乏而无法有效建模。

Method: 1) 采用LinUCB上下文在线学习框架应对数据稀疏；2) 用Cholesky因子的直接秩1更新替代Sherman-Morrison矩阵求逆，保证协方差矩阵正定性及数值稳定性；3) 集成Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）降低推理延迟，利用特征空间语义冗余。

Result: 基于Tagesschau API的实证评估表明，该方法在保持排序精度不显著下降的前提下，实现了4.85倍的效率提升。

Conclusion: Kairos为资源与数据受限环境下的高性能推荐系统提供了一个可行蓝图，验证了上下文在线学习结合数值稳定优化策略在解决新闻冷启动问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Algorithmic news personalization in regional markets often fails because modern deep learning models require massive interaction data while real-world news has a short Time-to-Live (TTL < 48 h) and shallow article pools. This structural item cold-start deprives collaborative filtering of the data needed for robust modeling. This paper presents Project Kairos, a framework that bridges this data scarcity through a contextual online learning approach (LinUCB). To ensure numerical integrity for continuous operation, Kairos replaces error-prone Sherman-Morrison inversions with direct rank-1 updates of Cholesky factors. This preserves the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix even under ill-conditioned data scenarios. Simultaneously, Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) integration addresses inference latency. Empirical evaluations based on the Tagesschau API demonstrate that exploiting semantic redundancy in the feature space achieves a 4.85-fold efficiency gain without significantly compromising ranking precision. Kairos thus provides a blueprint for high-performance recommendation systems in resource- and data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [186] [Tight Generalization Bound for AdaBoost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26838)
*Mikael Møller Høgsgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了AdaBoost的泛化误差为Θ((d ln(nγ²/d))/(nγ²) + ln(1/δ)/n)，其中γ是弱学习器的优势，d是弱假设类的VC维，n是样本量，δ是置信参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在给出AdaBoost算法泛化误差的严格上界，填补理论分析中的空白。

Method: 结合AdaBoost输出的投票分类器其投票函数具有零经验γ/2-间隔损失这一已知事实，与一种新的基于间隔的投票分类器泛化界。

Result: 得到了AdaBoost泛化误差的紧界（上界为本工作贡献，匹配下界来自先前工作）。

Conclusion: 给出了AdaBoost泛化误差的精确刻画，证明了其与弱学习器优势γ、VC维d、样本量n和置信参数δ之间的定量关系。

Abstract: In this paper we show that the generalization error of AdaBoost is $Θ\big(\tfrac{d\ln(nγ^{2}/d)}{nγ^2}+\tfrac{\ln(1/δ)}{n}\big)$, where $γ$ is the advantage guaranteed by the weak learner, $d$ is the VC-dimension of the class containing the weak hypotheses, $n$ is the sample size, and $δ$ is the confidence parameter. The contribution of this paper is the upper bound; the matching lower bound follows from prior work. The upper bound proof follows by combining the known fact that AdaBoost outputs a voting classifier whose voting function has zero empirical $γ/2$-margin loss with what is, to the best of our knowledge, a new margin-based generalization bound for voting classifiers.

</details>


### [187] [Thinking Under Uncertainty: Evidence Use and Information-Seeking in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26845)
*Hua-Dong Xiong,Xinyuan Yan,Ji-An Li,Jingming Xue,Marcelo G. Mattar,Robert C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在双臂老虎机任务中比较了大型语言模型的推理时思考模式与非思考模式，发现思考能增强基于价值的行动并减少与不确定性无关的选择噪声，但并未促进信息寻求行为（如UCB式探索或汤普森式探索）。思考长度在不平衡历史条件下增加，报告置信度对决策难度更敏感且与任务证据关联更强。解码器扫描（尤其是温度）改变了选择噪声和思考长度，但无法复现跨输出的联合模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注推理时思考能否提升LLM的整体表现，但未能区分这种提升是由于模型更有效地利用已有证据，还是由于模型主动寻求有助于未来决策的新信息。本文旨在通过受控的实验设计，分离这两种机制，从而更精细地理解推理时思考对决策过程的真实影响。

Method: 10个开源LLM在匹配的眼界式双臂老虎机任务中完成思考和思考两种模式下的实验。采用认知模型将价值导向行动和与不确定性无关的选择噪声从两种探索行为中分离出来：对较少探索臂的UCB式偏好和随总不确定性增加的汤普森式选择变异性。比较不同模式下的行动偏好、思考长度和报告置信度。

Result: （1）思考增强了价值导向行动并减少了与不确定性无关的选择噪声；（2）思考未产生UCB式探索，也未加强汤普森式探索；（3）信息不平衡历史条件（观察次数更多）伴随更长的思考长度；（4）报告置信度对决策难度更敏感，且与所选任务证据关联更强；（5）解码器参数（尤其是温度）改变了选择噪声和思考长度，但无法复现跨输出的联合模式。

Conclusion: 在受控决策任务中，推理时思考改进了模型基于当前证据行动的能力，但并未支持一种转向更主动寻求信息的策略的结论。思考长度和置信度模式分别与元认知控制和元认知监测一致，但未能确立这些过程的存在。

Abstract: Inference-time thinking improves the performance of large language models, but aggregate outcomes do not reveal whether models use available evidence more effectively or seek information that could improve future decisions. We distinguish these responses by measuring action preference, thinking length, and reported confidence under matched uncertainty. Ten open-weight models completed matched horizon-style two-armed bandit trials in thinking and non-thinking modes. A cognitive model separated value-guided action and uncertainty-independent choice noise from two behavioral signatures of exploration: a UCB-like preference for the less-known arm and Thompson-like choice variability that increases with total uncertainty. On average, thinking strengthened value-guided action and reduced uncertainty-independent choice noise, without producing UCB-like exploration or strengthening Thompson-like exploration. Outside action, the information-imbalanced history condition, which also displayed more observations than the matched balanced condition, was associated with greater thinking length. Reported confidence became more sensitive to decision difficulty and more strongly associated with chosen task evidence. We interpret these thinking-length and reported-confidence patterns as consistent with metacognitive control and metacognitive monitoring, respectively, without establishing either process. Decoder sweeps, especially temperature, altered choice noise and thinking length but did not reproduce the joint cross-output pattern. In this controlled decision setting, thinking improved how models acted on current evidence, while neither measured signature supported a shift toward a more information-seeking policy.

</details>


### [188] [TREA-Net: A Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for Dengue Incidence Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26854)
*Inesh Shukla,Madhurima Panja,Tanujit Chakraborty,Chittaranjan Hens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREA-Net是一种轻量级迁移学习框架，通过环境时间序列SIR模型的残差校正，在数据匮乏地区实现精准的多周登革热预测，在10个迁移设置中9个获得显著提升，并将预测区间宽度缩小29.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新建登革热监测系统缺乏历史数据，难以训练可靠的神经预测模型；预训练时序模型虽提供零样本预测，但其跨领域训练无法捕捉本地流行病动力学特征。

Method: 提出TREA-Net，包含三个核心设计：(1) 环境时序SIR模型投影增强神经预测骨干网络；(2) 轻量级门控残差校正模块，可从数据丰富区域迁移至数据稀缺区域；(3) 节点不变设计适配不同数量的监测位置，目标域仅需学习两个全局参数。

Result: 从哥伦比亚和尼加拉瓜迁移至墨西哥和马来西亚，仅用78或104周目标数据实现8周预测；在5种神经骨干网络和10种迁移设置中，9个设置性能提升；集成TiRex基础模型后在所有目标数据集上达到最低MAE；保形预测将8周预测区间宽度缩减29.6%。

Conclusion: TREA-Net作为一种轻量级、可移植的早期预警框架，适用于监测数据有限的卫生机构，有效提升了登革热预测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate multi-week dengue forecasting supports timely vector-control interventions, outbreak preparedness, and healthcare resource allocation. However, newly established surveillance systems often lack the historical data needed to train reliable neural forecasting models. Although pretrained time-series models offer promising zero-shot forecasts, their cross-domain training may not capture local epidemiological dynamics. We propose TREA-Net, a Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for dengue forecasting under limited data. TREA-Net augments neural forecasting backbones with projections from an Environmental Time-Series Susceptible-Infected-Recovered model and learns a lightweight gated residual correction transferable from data-rich to data-scarce regions. Its node-invariant design accommodates surveillance systems with different numbers of locations, while target adaptation requires learning only two global parameters. We transfer knowledge from long-running dengue surveillance in Colombia and Nicaragua to 8-week-ahead forecasting in Mexico and Malaysia using only 78 or 104 weeks of target data. Across five neural backbones and ten transfer settings, TREA-Net improves the corresponding backbone in 9 out of 10 settings, with statistically significant gains. When integrated with TiRex, a foundation model for forecasting, it achieves the lowest mean absolute error across all target datasets. Conformal prediction further maintains empirical coverage while reducing 8-week prediction-interval width by 29.6% in Mexico. These results demonstrate TREA-Net's potential as a lightweight and portable early-warning framework for health agencies with limited surveillance data.

</details>


### [189] [Amortized Moment Matching for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26860)
*Wenze Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出摊销矩匹配（Amortized Moment Matching）框架，利用神经网络学习数据矩作为分布训练信号。通过多项式投影将扩散降噪器转化为矩摊销模型，推导出摊销Fréchet距离（AMFD）损失。AMFD采用无矩阵的交替优化流程动态学习条件矩，可扩展至高维数据。在全局表征特征上作为后训练目标时，其神经公式化比精确统计匹配更稳健，在FDr⁶指标上显著超越FD基线，在ImageNet上实现优越的一步生成。在文生图任务中，AMFD的条件感知特性大幅提升指令遵循能力，使一步模型在GenEval基准上超越多步教师模型FLUX.2 4B，在PickScore上达到持平水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Fréchet距离（FD）的分布匹配损失依赖于显式边缘矩计算，难以高效扩展至高维数据，且训练动态不够稳健。作者旨在提出一种可动态学习条件矩的神经网络框架，克服显式统计匹配的局限，实现可扩展、稳健的分布匹配训练。

Method: 1) 通过多项式投影将扩散降噪器参数化为矩摊销模型，建立n次投影显式识别n+1阶数据矩的理论框架。2) 从可解仿射情况出发，推导出摊销Fréchet距离（AMFD）损失。3) 采用无矩阵的交替优化流程，动态学习条件均值和协方差。4) 在全局表征特征上作为后训练目标应用，并在文生图场景中利用条件感知特性提升指令遵循能力。

Result: 1) 在FDr⁶指标上显著超越FD基线。2) 在ImageNet上实现优越的一步生成。3) 指出前两阶矩仅在具有强语义的空间中才能识别目标分布。4) 在文生图任务中，一步模型在GenEval上超越多步FLUX.2 4B教师模型，在PickScore上达到持平水平。

Conclusion: 摊销矩匹配框架通过神经网络的动态条件矩学习，有效克服了传统显式统计匹配的局限，在图像生成和文生图任务中均展现出更优的性能和可扩展性，尤其条件感知特性为一步生成模型带来了显著的指令遵循能力提升。

Abstract: We propose amortized moment matching, utilizing neural networks to learn data moments as distributional training signals. By casting diffusion denoisers through polynomial projections, we establish a general framework for moment amortization, revealing that an $n$-th degree projection explicitly identifies data moments up to order $n+1$. Derived from the tractable affine case, we instantiate the Amortized Fréchet Distance (AMFD) loss. Unlike FD-loss which relies on explicit marginal moment calculations, AMFD is able to dynamically learn conditional moments via an alternating, matrix-free optimization pipeline that effortlessly scales to high-dimensional data. When operating on global representation features, AMFD serves as a powerful post-training objective; empirically, its neural formulation yields more robust training dynamics than exact statistical matching, substantially surpassing the FD baseline on the FDr$^6$ metric and achieving superior one-step generation on ImageNet. Furthermore, it unlocks direct exploration within native generative spaces, suggesting that the first two moments can identify target distributions only in spaces with strong semantics. Finally, when scaled to text-to-image generation, the condition-aware nature of AMFD unlocks massive gains in instruction-following capabilities, enabling our one-step models to outperform their multi-step FLUX.2 [klein] 4B teachers on the GenEval benchmark while achieving on-par performance on PickScore. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/poppuppy/amfd.

</details>


### [190] [ReCo: Reweighting GRPO Against Distributional Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26862)
*Junoh Park,Junseo Hwang,Wonguk Cho,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO在强化学习中会导致模型覆盖推理路径减少，这是因为高概率响应和token级重要性比率的集中效应。本文提出ReCo方法，通过对响应出现次数进行归一化和用方差比替代重要性比率来缓解这一问题，在多个数学推理任务上改善了Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现GRPO会降低基础模型的推理能力，特别是在k值较大时Pass@k表现不如基础模型，这表明GRPO减少了推理路径的覆盖范围。本文旨在探究这一问题的成因并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出ReCo（Reweighting方法），包含两个关键机制：(1) 响应级：对响应贡献按其在该轮rollout组中的期望出现次数进行归一化；(2) token级：用基于方差的比率替代原始重要性比率，使得对非饱和决策点（即还存在合理备选token的点）给予更大的更新尺度。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，针对五个数学推理基准，ReCo在大k值下显著提升Pass@k，在小k值时与GRPO表现相当。

Conclusion: GRPO的推理路径覆盖减少源于响应级和token级的双重集中效应，ReCo通过重新加权有效缓解了这一问题，实现了更好的推理路径覆盖与性能权衡。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a standard reinforcement learning method for post-training language models. Recent work shows that GRPO can reduce the base model's reasoning capacity and underperform it in Pass@k when k is large, indicating reduced coverage of reasoning paths. We find that this reduction is associated with GRPO concentrating on responses that the base model already generates with high probability. We trace this concentration to two mechanisms in the GRPO update. At the response level, high-probability responses dominate the group gradient through repeated occurrence. At the token level, GRPO's importance ratio scales gradients, further reinforcing tokens that become more likely under the current policy. We propose ReCo, a reweighting method that addresses both effects. Response contributions are normalized by their expected occurrence within the rollout group, and the token-level importance ratio is replaced with a variance-based ratio that gives larger update scale to non-saturated decision points where alternative token choices remain plausible. Across Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ReCo improves Pass@k for large values of k and is comparable to GRPO for small values of k.

</details>


### [191] [Actions Have Consequences: Detecting Outcome Performativity using Intervention Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26908)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPAB的检测方法，用于识别预测结果会因果影响其自身预测结果的"结果表现性"现象，通过比较不同预测干预组的结果分布差异来实现检测，并给出了样本复杂度边界和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多领域（如姑息治疗、信用分配和推荐系统），预测可能会因果性地影响其所预测的结果，这种现象被称为"结果表现性"。然而，目前缺乏形式化的方法来检测这种表现性现象。

Method: 提出了结果表现性A/B检测（OPAB）方法，通过评估不同预测组（干预组）之间的结果分布差异来检测结果表现性。如果差异显著，则检测到结果表现性。推导了在不同结果表现性假设类别下的样本复杂度边界。

Result: 实验结果表明OPAB在多种情况下能够有效检测结果表现性。同时发现了"不可区分区域"，即在分配的干预数量不足时无法检测结果表现性。在Open Bandits数据集上的案例研究验证了OPAB的有效性。

Conclusion: OPAB为检测结果表现性提供了一种形式化方法，但在样本稀缺、成本高昂或获取样本不符合伦理的场景中，其检测能力受到限制。该工作对结果表现性的可检测性具有更广泛的实践意义。

Abstract: In many domains such as Palliative Care, Credit Assignment and Recommender Systems, predictions may causally influence the outcomes they predict. This phenomena is known as Outcome Performativity. This paper formalises an approach for detecting Outcome Performativity using prediction intervention called Outcome Performativity A/B Detection (OPAB). OPAB enables the detection of Outcome Performativity by assessing the dissimilarity in outcome distributions produced by different predictions groups (interventions). If that dissimilarity is significant, Outcome Performativity is detected. We derive sample complexity bounds for OPAB under various Outcome Performative assumption classes which we empirically validate. Results show that detecting Outcome Performativity using OPAB is achievable in numerous cases. Results also show the presence of regions of indistinguishability which describe settings where the allotted number of interventions are insufficient for detecting Outcome Performativity. The results of which have broader practical implications for the detectability of Outcome Performativity in settings where samples are scarce, cost-prohibitive or potentially unethical to obtain. The paper concludes with a case study on the efficacy of OPAB on the Open Bandits dataset, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [192] [Two Calls Beat Five Agents: Evaluating Multi-Agent Pipelines Against Self-Refinement for Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26922)
*Ashish Prajapati,Om Mohite*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在本地7B模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上部署多智能体LLM管道系统Parishad的效果。结果表明：在GSM8K上，多智能体系统因错误累积导致准确率下降（从75%降至45%），改用纯文本格式后恢复至82%；而简单的两轮自优化策略（V1）达到86.2%且token使用量降低7.4倍。在HumanEval上（直接准确率已达96.3%），多智能体或V1策略反而破坏性能（66.5%），任务感知门控重设计（V2）可保持95.1%。结论：通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度更关键，简单方法在本地7B模型上可匹配或超越多智能体管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM管道系统虽能通过任务分解提升推理能力，但现有基准测试主要使用大规模商业模型，缺乏在本地小规模模型上的系统评估，且未关注通信格式和实现细节对性能的影响。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct本地模型上部署Parishad（五角色多智能体系统），在GSM8K（500题）和HumanEval（164题）上实验，对比直接提示、两轮自优化（V1）和任务感知门控重设计（V2），并测试JSON与纯文本两种通信格式。

Result: GSM8K：多智能体系统在JSON格式下准确率从75%降至45%（错误累积），纯文本格式恢复至82%；V1自优化策略达86.2%且token用量降低7.4倍。HumanEval：直接准确率96.3%，V1降至66.5%，V2门控设计保持95.1%。

Conclusion: 通信格式和实现细节对性能的影响大于架构复杂度；对于本地7B模型部署，简单方法（如自优化）在匹配或超越多智能体管道性能的同时，能大幅降低token开销。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipeline systems break down the task among multiple roles for better reasoning, but are benchmarked mainly with large-scale commercial models. In this study, we investigate Parishad, a structured multi-agent system involving five roles, by deploying it on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a local model, on two datasets: GSM8K (500 questions) and HumanEval (164 questions), compared with prompting directly and two-call self-refinement. The multi-agent system drops GSM8K accuracy from 75.0\% to 45.0\% with JSON data format due to the error accumulation problem. With plaintext format, the accuracy is restored to 82.0\%. A two-call self-refinement strategy (V1) can achieve 86.2\% accuracy on GSM8K, with 7.4$\times$ lower token usage. However, the same V1 implementation on HumanEval---where direct accuracy is already 96.3\%---actively destroys performance (66.5\%). A task-aware gated redesign (V2) applied to HumanEval preserves accuracy at 95.1\%. Our results demonstrate that communication format and implementation details determine outcomes more than architectural complexity, and that simpler approaches match or outperform multi-agent pipelines for local 7B model deployment. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [193] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LeWM中的边际高斯正则化（SIGReg）在多任务学习中会导致潜在表示聚类间的混淆，提出将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差而非边际分布，在LIBERO基准上提升了1.7倍长序列成功率，平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%，揭示了边际高斯先验与多任务潜在结构之间的不兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM中的SIGReg方法在单任务场景下效果良好，但扩展到多任务训练时性能显著下降，行为克隆效果变差。本文旨在分析该问题的成因并给出解决方案。

Method: 将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差（temporally centered residuals）而非原始潜在边际分布，避免对聚类中心间距的直接正则化压力，同时保留抗坍缩效果。

Result: LIBERO基准上，长序列任务成功率提升1.7倍，四套套件平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%；无外部预训练时略优于从头训练的Diffusion Policy，接近大规模预训练策略基线。

Conclusion: 边际高斯先验与多任务潜在结构存在结构性不兼容，时间中心化残差上的SIGReg提供了一种稳定且可扩展的多任务端到端世界模型学习途径。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [194] [Surrogate assisted diversity estimation in neural ensemble search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26940)
*Alexandr Udeneev,Petr Babkin,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双目标代理引导的神经集成搜索方法，通过训练两个代理模型分别估计预测精度和多样性潜力，高效地识别既个体强又集体多样的架构组合，在多个数据集上取得优于基准方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经架构搜索（NAS）方法计算成本高昂，将其扩展至神经集成搜索（NES）会导致搜索空间指数级增长，使得问题在计算上变得难以处理。因此需要一种高效的方法来同时优化个体架构及其集成组成。

Method: 将候选架构表示为有向无环图（DAG），独立训练两个代理模型分别估计预测精度和多样性潜力，结合两者的估计结果引导NES框架，高效识别既个体强又集体多样的架构组合。

Result: 在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，最终集成取得了与Deep Ensembles和Random Search等标准基线相比具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 双目标代理引导的方法能够在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，有效解决神经集成搜索中搜索空间爆炸的问题，生成高质量且多样化的集成模型。

Abstract: Ensembles are a standard way to improve the performance and robustness of deep neural networks, but their effectiveness crucially depends on both the quality and the diversity of individual models. Most neural architecture search (NAS) methods are computationally expensive. Extending them to neural ensemble search (NES), which requires joint optimization of individual architectures and their ensemble composition, leads to an exponential growth of the search space and makes the problem computationally intractable. To address this, we introduce a dual-objective surrogate-guided ensemble search: candidate architectures are represented as directed acyclic graphs, and two surrogate models are trained independently to estimate predictive accuracy and diversity potential. Their combined estimates guide an NES framework that efficiently identifies architectures that are both individually strong and collectively diverse. Our final ensemble achieves competitive or superior performance compared to standard baselines such as Deep Ensembles and Random Search on FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [195] [Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Unified Linear-Probing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26993)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了24种预训练视觉基础模型在冻结状态下（仅训练线性分类头）进行人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）的性能，发现这些模型在数据集内表现良好，但跨数据集泛化能力有限，表明预训练表示包含PAD相关信息，但显式域适应仍必不可少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集评估下人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）因域偏移而性能退化，且大规模标注数据稀缺，因此需要探究通用视觉基础模型是否已在预训练表示中编码了PAD相关信息，从而可以通过最小化任务特定训练实现有效检测。

Method: 在MCIO基准（包含MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU四个数据集）上，采用统一的线性探测协议，冻结24种编码器（包括自监督ViT、视觉-语言编码器和监督CNN）的骨干网络，仅训练轻量线性分类头，以隔离预训练表示中已有的PAD信息，并与两个专用PAD基线进行比较。

Result: 冻结的基础模型表示在数据集内可实现强PAD性能（仅需线性分类器），但无法可靠地跨数据集迁移；模型规模在部分族内有益，但效果非单调且受架构和预训练方式强烈影响；InternViT-6B取得最低平均数据集内错误率，CLIP ViT-B/32在跨数据集迁移-计算权衡中最优。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉基础模型包含PAD相关信息，但要解决域偏移问题，显式域适应仍然是必要的。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) remains challenging under cross-dataset evaluation, where domain shift degrades models trained on a single dataset. The scarcity of large-scale labeled data motivates adapting pretrained vision models rather than training task-specific architectures from scratch, raising a fundamental question: do general-purpose vision foundation models encode PAD-relevant information accessible with minimal task-specific training? To investigate, we systematically evaluate 24 frozen encoders, including self-supervised vision transformers, vision-language encoders, and supervised CNNs, using a unified linear-probing protocol on the MCIO benchmark (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU). The backbone remains fixed, and only a lightweight linear head is trained to isolate the PAD information already present in the pretrained representation. %We report intra- and cross-dataset performance, along with accuracy-compute trade-offs, relative to two specialist PAD baselines. Results show that frozen foundation-model representations can support strong intra-dataset PAD performance with only a linear classifier, but this performance does not reliably transfer across datasets. Model scale is beneficial within several families, although the effect is not monotonic and is strongly mediated by architecture and pretraining. InternViT-6B achieves the lowest mean intra-dataset error, whereas CLIP ViT-B/32 offers the most favorable cross-dataset transfer-compute trade-off among the evaluated probes. These findings suggest that while pretrained representations contain PAD-relevant information, explicit adaptation remains necessary to address domain shift.

</details>


### [196] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过POKEWORLD和RH20T实验证明，潜世界模型中的物理参数学习由预测目标决定而非仅仅输入数据；只有被预测目标明确需要的物理量才会被表征，且数据量增加仅能改进已被学习的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究潜世界模型中哪些物理量被表征以及决定因素，区分环境限制和客观限制。

Method: 使用POKEWORLD环境进行受控干预实验，采用证书门控协议验证参数可恢复性；在RH20T上进行交叉验证。

Result: 刚度仅在预测触觉时进入潜变量（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）；阻力在所有确定性预测目标下均不超过0.13，而监督头达0.45；RH20T上仅完整多模态目标能预测力。

Conclusion: 目标结构决定潜变量获取的物理参数，数据量增加仅改进已获取的参数。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [197] [TreeCCA: Canonical Correlation Analysis via Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27027)
*James Chapman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeCCA是首个将梯度提升树作为CCA编码器端到端训练的方法，利用Eckart-Young损失实现即插即用，在非线性精度、可解释性和计算效率上超越或匹配深度CCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型相关分析（CCA）长期以来依赖线性或神经编码器，缺乏梯度提升树这类在表格数据中占主导地位的即插即用方法，且神经编码器可解释性差。

Method: 提出Eckart-Young（EY）损失函数，为每个样本提供闭式梯度，可直接作为自定义目标函数嵌入XGBoost/LightGBM等标准GBT库中，实现树模型端到端CCA训练。

Result: 在合成基准上匹配或超越深度CCA（Signed Power: 2.61 vs 2.43; Hermite: 2.93 vs 2.89）；稀疏基准上Precision@S=1.00（p=50）而PMD无信号；UCI HAR上以5倍低成本达到可比精度；五个多视图数据集上一致匹配或超越线性CCA。

Conclusion: TreeCCA首次将梯度提升树引入CCA框架，兼具非线性精度与原生可解释性（树分裂特征选择即提供增益重要性），为表格多视图分析提供高效、可靠且可解释的新范 paradigm。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees dominate tabular machine learning, yet canonical correlation analysis has always relied on linear or neural encoders. We propose \textbf{TreeCCA}, the first method to train gradient-boosted tree ensembles end-to-end as CCA encoders, inheriting their plug-and-play reliability: no architecture design, familiar hyperparameters, and strong performance with defaults. The technical enabler is the Eckart-Young (EY) loss, which supplies closed-form per-sample gradients that slot directly into any standard GBT library (XGBoost, LightGBM) as a custom objective.
  TreeCCA is the first CCA method to combine nonlinear accuracy with native interpretability: every tree split selects one feature, so gain importances reveal which inputs drive cross-view correlation at no extra cost. We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [198] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定下游决策逻辑的实际部署条件下，稀疏模型（包括彩票票据）能否无缝替换密集模型。实验发现，尽管稀疏模型能恢复密集模型的准确率，但其行为存在显著差异，导致7%-10%的决策变更，揭示了仅凭干净准确率认证的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据对模型行为影响的结论不一致（既有有益也有有害），且未考虑实际部署场景中决策逻辑已固定的情况。作者希望从实际部署角度评估这些混合发现。

Method: 在部署层面研究生产替换问题：用精度匹配的彩票票据或其他稀疏模型替换原有密集模型，而不重新配置下游决策逻辑。审计了校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表征和下游策略决策等一系列行为，并用行为兼容距离总结除干净准确率外的偏差。

Result: 稀疏候选模型能恢复密集参考模型的准确率，但行为上仍存在显著差异；在多个场景中彩票票据显示更低的损坏准确率；在固定阈值策略诊断下，彩票票据替换导致7%-10%的接受-审查决策变更。

Conclusion: 建立了干净准确率认证的局限性：与固定原模型兼容与将变更归因于稀疏性或视每个偏差为有害是不同的。理论解释了即使精确的逐点top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变化，操作边界附近的小置信度偏移会产生一阶路由变更。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [199] [CoCaRS: Correlation Calibration-Based Redundancy Suppression for Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27054)
*Fengming Yu,Haiwei Pan,Kejia Zhang,Chunling Chen,Jian Guan,Baoying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCaRS方法，通过困惑证据估计（CEE）和强度分配控制（SAC）校准特征去相关以保留结构信息，并引入自适应系数调节（ACR）降低对超参数设置的敏感性，有效提升了异构知识蒸馏的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于冗余抑制的异构知识蒸馏方法存在两个问题：1）均匀去相关可能削弱有用的结构信息；2）固定的冗余抑制系数对不同师生模型对和训练阶段的敏感性较高，导致蒸馏效果不稳定。

Method: 提出CoCaRS方法，包含三个核心组件：1）Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) - 捕获可靠的语义关系用于相关估计；2）Strength Allocation Control (SAC) - 在去相关过程中保留判别性结构；3）Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) - 根据相对损失规模自适应调节校准后的冗余抑制目标的贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K数据集上的大量实验验证了CoCaRS在提升蒸馏性能和降低对系数设置敏感性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CoCaRS通过校准去相关过程和自适应调节系数，有效解决了异构知识蒸馏中冗余抑制方法的两大局限性，为异构模型蒸馏提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables a compact student model to learn from a powerful teacher and has become an effective paradigm for model compression. The emergence of diverse model architectures has extended KD from homogeneous to heterogeneous settings. However, differences in architectural inductive biases between the teacher and student models often result in substantial representation discrepancies, limiting the effectiveness of direct knowledge transfer. Recently, redundancy suppression has offered a new perspective on heterogeneous KD by preserving cross-architecture invariance and reducing feature redundancy through decorrelation of teacher-student feature correlations. Nevertheless, this formulation may weaken useful structural information through uniform decorrelation, while a fixed coefficient may make the effective contribution of redundancy suppression sensitive to teacher-student pairs and training stages. To address these problems, Correlation Calibration-based Redundancy Suppression (CoCaRS) is proposed to better retain structural information while suppressing redundancy and reduce sensitivity to coefficient settings across teacher-student pairs and training stages. Specifically, CoCaRS calibrates feature decorrelation through Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) and Strength Allocation Control (SAC), which respectively capture reliable semantic relations for correlation estimation and preserve discriminative structure during decorrelation. Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) further regulates the contribution of the calibrated redundancy suppression objective according to its relative loss scale, reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1K validate the effectiveness of CoCaRS in improving distillation performance and reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [200] [Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Online Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27073)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HT-PAder算法，结合重启AdaGrad专家与AdaGrad-Hedge元算法，在重尾噪声的非平稳环境下实现了无参数、最优的动态遗憾界。该算法不需要提前知道任何问题参数，并在p=2（有限方差）时首次给出无参数的极小极大通用动态遗憾保证，同时证明了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境下在线凸优化(OCO)面临重尾噪声挑战，随机梯度仅具有有限p阶中心矩(p∈(1,2])。静态遗憾已被充分研究，但实现无参数的通用动态遗憾仍是一个未解决的开放问题。

Method: 提出HT-PAder算法，核心包含两个组件：(1) 在几何划分的块长度上运行重启AdaGrad专家；(2) 使用AdaGrad-Hedge作为路径元算法，该元算法对元损失没有任何矩条件要求。算法不需要预先知道任何问题参数（如域直径D、Lipschitz常数G、噪声水平σ、比较器路径长度P_T）。

Result: 期望通用动态遗憾界为：Õ(GD√(T(1+P_T/D)) + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p})。当p=2时，首次给出无参数的极小极大通用动态遗憾保证；同时证明了匹配的路径长度指数下界，验证了最优性。

Conclusion: HT-PAder成功解决了重尾噪声下非平稳OCO的无参数通用动态遗憾问题，达到了最优的路径长度指数，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization (OCO) in non-stationary environments under heavy-tailed noise, where the stochastic gradient oracle admits only a finite $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1, 2]$. While static regret is well-understood, achieving universal dynamic regret in a parameter-free manner remains an open challenge. We resolve this by proposing \textbf{HT-PAder}, a parameter-free algorithm combining restarted AdaGrad experts over a geometric pool of block lengths with a pathwise meta-algorithm, \textbf{AdaGrad-Hedge}, which requires no moment conditions on meta-losses. For a domain of diameter $D$, Lipschitz constant $G$, noise level $σ$, and comparator path length $P_T$, HT-PAder achieves an expected universal dynamic regret of \[ \widetilde O\left( GD\sqrt{T(1+P_T/D)} + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p} \right). \] The algorithm does not require prior knowledge of any of these problem parameters. Even in the special case of finite variance ($p=2$), HT-PAder provides the first parameter-free minimax universal dynamic regret guarantee. We also prove a matching lower bound, establishing the optimality of the path-length exponent.

</details>


### [201] [Single-Beat Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation Using Ear-PPG and ECG with a Lightweight Hybrid Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27076)
*Kindeep K. Dhatt,Tengyue Wu,Hanbang Hua,Yayun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级多模态可穿戴框架，实现基于单次心搏的连续无袖带血压估计。该框架同步胸部ECG与耳夹式反射PPG信号，结合混合学习架构（1D-CNN提取PPG嵌入 + 30个生理特征 + LightGBM回归），在10名受试者的多阶段压力协议和PulseDB公开数据集上取得SBP MAE 4.02 mmHg、DBP MAE 1.79 mmHg的结果，较基线模型综合MAE降低28.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续无袖带血压监测方法受运动伪影、生理变异性及传统PTT模型鲁棒性不足的困扰；多数方法依赖多秒时间窗口来稳定估计，但在实际监测中信号会间歇性损坏，导致该假设频繁失效。本文旨在证明血压相关信息可在单次心搏层面保持，并开发适合可穿戴设备部署的轻量级高效框架。

Method: 1) 硬件：同步胸部ECG与耳夹式反射PPG，各配6轴IMU提供运动上下文；2) 特征提取：1D-CNN从单个PPG心搏提取64维嵌入，融合30个基于生理的特征（含PTT统计量和HRV）；3) 回归：LightGBM回归模型；4) 评估：10名受试者的多阶段压力协议 + PulseDB公开数据集，30次独立运行的受试者分离验证。

Result: SBP MAE 4.02±0.21 mmHg，DBP MAE 1.79±0.05 mmHg，较基线模型综合MAE降低28.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现无需长时间上下文的单次心搏血压估计，支持在计算资源受限的可穿戴设备上高效部署连续无袖带血压监测。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [202] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于并行轨迹回火(PTT)的训练算法，通过在训练过程中维持平衡采样，解决了能量基模型(EBM)中马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合差的问题，实现了高效、稳定的最大似然训练，在受限玻尔兹曼机和离散表格数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量基模型(EBM)为科学数据生成建模提供了可解释框架，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)混合性能差严重限制了其可靠性和实用性，现有训练方法难以处理高度多模态和数据稀缺的科学数据集。

Method: 提出并行轨迹回火(Parallel Trajectory Tempering, PTT)训练算法，利用优化路径的连续性在整个学习过程中维持平衡采样；结合储层采样(reservoir sampling)和自适应优化，计算成本与持久对比散度(Persistent Contrastive Divergence)相当。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机(RBM)上，PTT持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上，超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成更高质量的样本，并对过拟合和有限数据具有更强的鲁棒性；能以几乎零额外成本提供热化时间估计、平衡样本和准确的对数似然值。

Conclusion: PTT使得EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，可作为标准训练方法的实际替代方案。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [203] [Scores Are Not Decisions: Cost-Aware Stopping for Tool Acquisition in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27083)
*Yicheng Feng,Yan Zhang,Yan Cheng,Wei Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAM-DF（成本感知边际决策聚焦停止）方法，解决LLM智能体在工具选择中面临的异构成本问题。该方法在排序后的工具前缀上做停止决策，直接优化当前停止与最优继续之间的离线差距，证明该目标与停止目标贝叶斯对齐，且纯评分规则在异构成本下非最优。在五个工具使用领域的1,343个任务上，CAM-DF在τ-bench Retail中达到最高收益，比预测-阈值基线在所有排序源和两个成本体制下均有提升。现场执行中，CAM-DF比完全访问减少37%的工具暴露，同时保持可比较的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖多种外部服务时面临根本性工具选择挑战：获取工具太少导致任务信息不足，获取太多增加成本、上下文负担和隐私暴露。现有路由器和检索器可以排序候选工具，但排序本身无法确定选择多少工具。现有方法未解决异构成本下的工具获取问题。

Method: 将工具选择决策形式化为成本感知边际决策聚焦停止（CAM-DF）问题，在排序后的工具前缀上做停止决策。提出CAM-DF-lite作为紧凑可解释变体。直接训练当前停止与最优继续之间的离线差距：其符号标记决策方向，其幅度加权每个错误的收益代价。证明该目标与停止目标贝叶斯对齐，以及纯评分规则在异构成本下非最优。

Result: 在五个工具使用领域的1,343个任务上评估。在τ-bench Retail中，CAM-DF在所有可部署方法中取得最高收益，在所有五个排序源和两个成本体制下均优于预测-阈值基线。在异构成本和高成本压力下达到最先进水平，在排名较弱时增益更大。现场执行中，CAM-DF比完全访问减少37%的工具暴露，同时保持可比较的任务成功率。

Conclusion: CAM-DF家族是一种轻量级预执行插件，可将现有工具排序转化为更低成本的获取决策，无需微调底层LLM。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly depend on diverse external services such as search engines, databases, and connectors, agent harnesses face a fundamental tool-selection challenge: acquiring too few tools leaves the task under-informed, while too many adds cost, context load, and privacy exposure. Routers and retrievers can rank candidate tools by relevance, but a ranking alone does not determine how many are worth selecting. Existing approaches leave acquisition under heterogeneous costs unaddressed. We formulate this decision as cost-aware marginal decision-focused stopping (CAM-DF) over ranked tool prefixes, with CAM-DF-lite as a compact interpretable variant. We train directly on the offline gap between stopping now and the best continuation: its sign labels the decision, its magnitude weights each error by the payoff at stake. We prove this objective is Bayes-aligned with the stopping target and that score-only rules are suboptimal under heterogeneous costs. We evaluate on 1,343 tasks across five tool-use domains. On $τ$-bench Retail, CAM-DF attains the highest payoff among deployable methods, with gains over a predict-then-threshold baseline across all five ranking sources and two cost regimes. Our approach is state-of-the-art under heterogeneous costs and high cost pressure, with larger gains under weaker rankings. In live execution, CAM-DF exposes the agent to 37\% fewer tools than full access while maintaining comparable task success. The CAM-DF family is a lightweight pre-execution plugin that turns existing tool rankings into lower-cost acquisition decisions without fine-tuning the underlying LLM.

</details>


### [204] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了~100B规模的语言模型是否在其残差流中包含可解码的夜空星图表示。发现大多数开源模型确实存在这种表示，在LOO测试中表现出显著分数，包含高达65-85%的方差，中位角误差低至12°-21°。这是首次发现的弯曲高维不可约特征流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否在其内部表征中隐式编码了关于宇宙/夜空的空间知识，验证模型是否具备超越简单文本关联的几何结构知识。

Method: 对~100B参数规模的开源语言模型进行分析，从残差流中解码夜空星图表示。使用LOO（留一法）测试评估表示的显著性，通过主成分分析（PCA）观察表示在顶层主成分中的显现，并计算R²分数和中位角误差来量化表示质量。

Result: 大多数开源模型在残差流中存在可解码的夜空星图表示；该表示在关于夜空关系的提示下会浮现到顶层主成分中；LOO测试中R²分数达65-85%，中位角误差为12°-21°；验证了该表示不是从相关平面表示中简单泄漏而来。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型内部存在弯曲高维不可约特征流形的夜空星图表示，这是首次发现此类几何结构的表征，表明模型学到了超越平面嵌入的复杂空间知识。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [205] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HierSTT，一种层次化Transformer框架，用于联合预测医院、区域和国家三个层面的急诊需求，实现一致性的多层级预测，在葡萄牙81家医院数据集上将WAPE降低32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ED需求预测方法多独立在单一层级进行，忽略医院-区域-国家之间的层级结构，导致跨层级预测不一致，无法有效支持多层级决策。

Method: 提出HierSTT层次化Transformer框架：Temporal Fusion Transformer捕捉国家级动态，时空Transformer编码器-解码器模块建模区域和医院层级的条件预测，采用一致性感知损失函数惩罚跨层级不一致性。

Result: 在葡萄牙81家医院、5个区域卫生管理局的数据集上，HierSTT相比最佳非层次化深度学习基线降低32% WAPE，优于所有经典层次化调和方法，并生成近乎一致的跨层级预测。

Conclusion: HierSTT通过端到端层次化建模有效解决了多层级ED预测的一致性问题，为医疗系统多层级决策提供了更可靠的预测支持。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [206] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Voronoi图的直方图作为期望持续图(EPD)的向量化方法，在无需显式光滑点变换模型的情况下，建立了稳定性边界并证明了其对Wasserstein尺度变化的保持能力，在真实数据集上展示了其在分类和降维任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图(PD)能有效捕捉点云拓扑，但计算时间复杂度高。期望持续图(EPD)通过研究点云多个子集的拓扑来降低时间成本，但现有EPD向量化方法（如高斯函数或景观函数）依赖预定义的点变换，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出基于Voronoi图的直方图作为EPD的向量化方法，用自适应划分的计数取代光滑函数逼近；在分离和归一化条件下建立稳定性边界，并刻画直方图表示保持Wasserstein尺度变化的特性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，该方法在分类和降维任务中表现出有效性，能够保持显著的拓扑特征。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图作为EPD的向量化方法，在无需显式光滑模型的前提下，提供了自适应的划分策略，具有良好的稳定性和拓扑特征保持能力。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [207] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对holonomy-cover决策过程（一种结构化POMDP），构建了稳定商（stable quotient）作为最小马尔可夫充分统计量，证明了当前观测与稳定类组合构成精确有限马尔可夫状态，并提出了Holonomy记忆强化学习框架，实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境下，智能体需要维护一个递归可更新的历史统计量来恢复马尔可夫性质，但最小这样的统计量通常是未知的。本文旨在刻画这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 针对holonomy-cover决策过程（可见动态是马尔可夫且每个可见转移对隐藏模式施加固定置换的结构化POMDP），构建稳定商（最粗的保持一步奖励和商后继的观测层面抽象），证明当前观测与稳定类的组合构成精确有限马尔可夫状态。在可达性和成对决策分离条件下，证明精确类追踪需要恰好最小记忆符号数。利用可重置诊断实现最近原型类推断，具有指数衰减误差。提出Holonomy记忆强化学习框架，通过有序边传输更新稳定类，识别局部类坐标，并在同步后应用标准有限MDP强化学习骨干。

Result: 实验从原始状态恢复出精确压缩到商状态，以三个决策时间记忆状态实现了完美的成对顺序准确率，与商预言机匹配并优于非预言机基线。

Conclusion: 稳定商是holonomy-cover决策过程中的最小马尔可夫充分统计量，Holonomy记忆强化学习框架能够有效利用这一结构实现高效学习。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [208] [Cost-Sensitive Conformal Prediction and Human-in-the-Loop Abstention for Imbalanced High-Stakes Decision Support: A Multi-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27143)
*Manpreet Singh,Akshatha Srikantha,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了边际共形预测在严重类别不平衡和高风险决策场景下对少数类覆盖不足的问题（少数类覆盖率低至0.5%），并通过15个数据集、7个分类模型、3种校准技术的3,150次实验证明，Mondrian（类别条件）共形预测可将少数类覆盖率平均提高61.7个百分点，结合成本控制弃权机制能显著降低期望决策成本，为分布自由、成本感知的不确定性量化提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策系统（如信用评分、欺诈检测、医疗诊断）面临严重的类别不平衡和不对称错误代价，而标准边际共形预测虽然在整体覆盖率上有保证，但在少数类上覆盖极差（低至0.5%），亟需一种能同时保证少数类覆盖并控制决策成本的方法。

Method: 在15个真实不平衡表格数据集上，对边际共形预测、Mondrian（类别条件）共形预测以及成本控制弃权机制进行综合基准比较，覆盖7种分类模型、3种概率校准技术、10个随机种子，共计3,150次实验运行。

Result: Mondrian共形预测将少数类覆盖率平均提升61.7个百分点（p < 1e-80）；结合成本控制弃权机制后，在现实人工审核预算下，期望决策成本显著低于标准决策边界、基于置信度的拒绝器和风险控制拒绝器；同时量化了数据集特定的盈亏平衡阈值。

Conclusion: Mondrian共形预测结合成本控制弃权机制能够有效解决不平衡数据下的少数类覆盖问题并降低决策成本，为高风险决策支持系统中部署分布自由、成本感知的不确定性量化提供了实用指南。

Abstract: High-stakes decision systems in credit scoring, fraud detection, healthcare, and industrial safety require reliable uncertainty quantification under severe class imbalance and asymmetric error costs. Standard marginal conformal prediction (CP) provides valid overall coverage guarantees; however, we show that it severely under-covers rare, costly minority classes, with minority-class coverage dropping to as low as 0.5% on certain datasets. To characterize and address this limitation, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark comparing marginal CP, class-conditional (Mondrian) CP, and cost-controlled abstention mechanisms across 15 real-world imbalanced tabular datasets, 7 classification models, 3 probability calibration techniques, and 10 random seeds, resulting in 3,150 experimental runs. Our results show that Mondrian CP restores valid minority-class coverage, achieving an average minority-coverage improvement of 61.7 percentage points over marginal CP (p < 1e-80). Furthermore, combining Mondrian CP with cost-controlled abstention significantly reduces expected decision cost compared with standard decision boundaries, confidence-based rejectors, and risk-controlled rejectors under realistic human review budgets. We further quantify dataset-specific break-even thresholds at which deferring ambiguous instances to human experts becomes cost-effective.
